# Agents · O · part 11

947 entries, osf-converter to overview. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **osf-converter**: Convert Markdown, plain text, HTML, or other document formats to OmniScript (OSF). Use when users want to convert existing documents to OSF format, such as "convert this markdown to OSF" or "turn this text into an OSF document". - **osp-answer-generator-agent**: Open ScholarPeer Answer Generator Agent — invoked as a subagent by the Query Agent (or as a self-reflection turn on the few tools without subagents). Receives a single probing question plus a minimal context bundle, performs verification against retrieved literature and domain narrative, and returns (answer, citations, discrepancy flag). Stateless across questions — context is supplied fresh each invocation. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.grok/agents/osp-answer-generator-agent.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)
- **osp-baseline-scout-agent**: Open ScholarPeer Baseline Scout Agent — adversarial auditor that identifies missing baselines and datasets the authors failed to compare against. Activate this persona when the user invokes /4-osp-baseline-scout. Operates with an intentionally skeptical posture — its job is to find omissions, not validate what's there. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.grok/agents/osp-baseline-scout-agent.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)
- **osp-historian-agent**: Open ScholarPeer Sub-Domain Historian Agent — compresses the retrieved literature into a chronological domain narrative. Activate this persona when the user invokes /3-osp-historian. Mimics the mental model of a senior researcher mapping the arc of progress in a sub-field. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.grok/agents/osp-historian-agent.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)
- **osp-literature-review-agent**: Open ScholarPeer Literature Review & Expansion Agent — performs External Context retrieval via the dynamic-web search strategy. Activate this persona when the user invokes /2-osp-literature. Runs up to three distinct rounds — the user chooses how many (sub-domain anchor, method anchor, temporal expansion) to construct the live reference frame C_dynamic. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.grok/agents/osp-literature-review-agent.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)
- **osp-orchestrator**: Open ScholarPeer top-level orchestrator. Activate this skill whenever the user invokes /open-scholar-peer, asks to review a paper, evaluate a manuscript, or references any /N-osp-* command. Owns the brain protocol: read .brain/session.json first, advise the user on the correct next step, and ensure phase ordering. Trigger phrases: "review this paper", "open scholar peer", "OSP", "what's next", "where am I in the review", or any /open-scholar-peer / /N-osp-* slash command. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.omp/agents/osp-orchestrator.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)
- **osp-query-agent**: Open ScholarPeer Query Agent — formulates probing questions targeting specific weaknesses of the paper. Activate this persona when the user invokes /5-osp-qa. Runs in the main thread; delegates each question to the Answer Generator Agent (subagent) on tools that support it, or self-reflects with strict turn markers on tools that don't. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.omp/agents/osp-query-agent.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)
- **osp-reviewer-agent**: Open ScholarPeer Reviewer Agent — synthesizes the structured summary, domain narrative, missing baselines, and verified Q&A pairs into a single consolidated review formatted to the venue's guidelines. Activate this persona when the user invokes /6-osp-review. Decoupled from investigation: this agent does no new retrieval, only synthesis. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.grok/agents/osp-reviewer-agent.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)
- **osp-summary-agent**: Open ScholarPeer Summary Agent — performs Internal Compression on the input paper. Activate this persona when the user invokes /1-osp-summary or asks to extract claims, method, or evidence from a paper. This is NOT a generic summarizer; it produces a review-oriented structured representation. ([amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer](https://github.com/amirkiarafiei/open-scholar-peer/blob/HEAD/extensions/.grok/agents/osp-summary-agent.md) · ★152 · 3 copies)

- **osprey-rule-debugger**: Use when fixing Osprey SML validation errors or reviewer-identified issues. Receives structured issue reports, diagnoses root causes, applies fixes across all issues in one pass, and runs osprey-cli as a self-check before reporting back. Commits fixes after each pass. Examples: "fix these validation errors", "resolve reviewer issues", "debug this SML error", "fix and re-validate". - **osprey-rule-impl**: Use when writing or modifying Osprey SML rule files from a validated rule specification. Receives a planner's rule spec and investigator's project context, then authors the actual SML: models, rules, effects, and execution graph wiring. Does not validate — that is the reviewer's job. Examples: "implement this rule spec", "write the SML for X", "create models and rules from this plan". - **osprey-rule-investigator**: Use when exploring an Osprey SML rules project to understand its structure, available labels, model hierarchy, UDF signatures, and execution graph. - **osprey-rule-planner**: Use when gathering requirements for a new Osprey SML rule before any code is written. Examples: "what should this rule detect", "plan a rule for X", "gather requirements for a harassment rule". - **osprey-rule-reviewer**: Use when validating or reviewing Osprey SML rules. Runs three-layer verification: osprey-cli validation, proactive pattern checks, and convention review. - **osprey-rule-writer**: Use this agent when working with Osprey SML moderation rules for atproto. - **oss-scout**: Находит репозитории-кандидаты открытого кода по теме и возвращает короткий список координат: `owner/repo`, одна строка «что именно там реализовано», признак живости. Разведка перед исследованием, а не исследование: код кандидатов не читает, выводов о пригодности не делает, сравнений не проводит. Используется фазой 1.6 скилла `research` и любым вызывающим, которому нужен список отправных точек до запуска дорогого сборщика. Не путать с `source-researcher` (`focus: oss-examples`) — тот изучает найденное и отчитывается о находках; этот только приносит имена. - **otel-collector**: OpenTelemetry 트레이스/메트릭 수집 점검·Grafana 대시보드 동기화. ([successbank/ultrav](https://github.com/successbank/ultrav/blob/HEAD/V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20%EC%97%90%EC%9D%B4%EC%A0%84%ED%8A%B8%20V9/dot-codex/agents/otel-collector.md))
- **otel-collector-engineer**: Use for **Phase B of the OSS observability stack** — OpenTelemetry collection and instrumentation. Owns the **OpenTelemetry Collector** pipeline (`receivers → processors → exporters`, **connectors** like `spanmetrics`, wired per-signal in `service.pipelines`), **OTLP** ingest (gRPC 4317 / HTTP 4318), processor ordering (**`memory_limiter` first, `batch` last**, `k8sattributes` / `resource` enrichment between), **tail-based sampling** (keep errors + slow traces, sample the rest — and the **load-balancing-exporter tier** required so all spans of a trace land on one collector), **semantic conven… ([lurodrisilva/personal-skills](https://github.com/lurodrisilva/personal-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/otel-collector-engineer.md))
- **otel-concurrency-reviewer**: Use when reviewing or writing changes to OpenTelemetry span processors, exporters, the tracer/provider bootstrap, or anything involving threads, asyncio, locks, or event loops in lumen-ai-core (tracer.py, sdk.py, processors/*, providers.py exporters, lumen-ai-celery instrumentor). Concurrency + OTel-lifecycle bugs are silent and severe here. ([skarL007/lumen-ai-sdk](https://github.com/skarL007/lumen-ai-sdk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/otel-concurrency-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **otel-config-builder**: Creates and validates OpenTelemetry collector configurations. Use when creating, modifying, or validating OTEL collector YAML configs, pipelines, receivers, processors, or exporters. ([aetorresdev/ai-minions](https://github.com/aetorresdev/ai-minions/blob/HEAD/agents/otel-config-builder.md) · ★3)
- **otel-go-expert**: Expert in OpenTelemetry for Go. Specializes in distributed tracing, metrics, logging, context propagation, exporters (OTLP, Jaeger, Prometheus), and instrumentation patterns. Use PROACTIVELY when adding observability to Go applications, debugging distributed systems, or setting up telemetry pipelines. ([zach-source/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/zach-source/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/claude/plugins/subagents/agents/otel-go-expert.md) · ★1)
- **otel-instrumentation**: OpenTelemetry instrumentation specialist. Sets up OTel SDK, Collector configuration, trace/metric/log pipelines, Prometheus exposition, Grafana dashboard templates, and SLO definitions. Use for services instrumented with OTel. ([TrevorEdris/fellowship-of-the-workflows](https://github.com/TrevorEdris/fellowship-of-the-workflows/blob/HEAD/platforms/agents/otel-instrumentation.md) · ★2)
- **otel-instrumentation**: Advises on OpenTelemetry (OTel) SDK instrumentation: span lifecycle and span surface design, attribute hygiene and semantic conventions, context propagation, metric instrument choice, and log/trace correlation. Spec-literate. Distinct from `otel-pipeline` (Collector and sampling), `observability-practice` (SLOs and alerts), `debuggability`. ([austintheriot/dotfiles](https://github.com/austintheriot/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/otel-instrumentation.md) · ★1)
- **otel-instrumentation-expert**: Investigate a codebase or stack and hand the main agent every input it needs to build a complete spec-driven plan for implementing OpenTelemetry instrumentation. Input - the path (or repo) to investigate and, if known, the export stack. Recalls previous investigations from .odd/otel-instrumentation-reports/ and persists its own report there (the odd-memory skill's otel-instrumentation-report reference), so expertise accumulates across SDD waves. Read-only against code - it never writes instrumentation code. ([using-system/oddyssey](https://github.com/using-system/oddyssey/blob/HEAD/.apm/agents/otel-instrumentation-expert.agent.md) · ★10 · 3 copies)
- **otel-log**: Investigate log errors, recurring patterns and source-code context in Berserk. ([berserkdb/berserk-skills](https://github.com/berserkdb/berserk-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/otel-log.md) · ★1)
- **otel-metric**: Discover OTel metrics and compute meaningful gauges, counter rates and histogram percentiles in Berserk. ([berserkdb/berserk-skills](https://github.com/berserkdb/berserk-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/otel-metric.md) · ★1)
- **otel-pipeline**: Advises on OpenTelemetry Collector configuration and telemetry pipeline design: agent/gateway topology, processors and OTTL, head vs tail sampling, cardinality management, and pipeline reliability and cost. Distinct from `otel-instrumentation` (SDK and spans), `observability-practice` (SLOs and alerts), `devops-infrastructure`. ([austintheriot/dotfiles](https://github.com/austintheriot/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/otel-pipeline.md) · ★1)
- **otel-python-expert**: Expert in OpenTelemetry for Python. Use PROACTIVELY when adding observability to Python applications, debugging distributed systems, or setting up telemetry pipelines. ([zach-source/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/zach-source/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/claude/plugins/subagents/agents/otel-python-expert.md) · ★1)

Use oteryn-platform-hard-worker instead for identity, game-boundary, money, migration or authorization changes. ([sr-7172/flight-risk](https://github.com/sr-7172/flight-risk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/overseer.md))
- **overseer**: Fresh-context verification agent — checks one claim, critique, or conclusion against its evidence before it propagates to the next pipeline step. The lab's confabulation circuit-breaker. ([Xarangi/lab-one](https://github.com/Xarangi/lab-one/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/overseer.md) · 2 copies)
- **overseer**: On-demand summary-layer advisor. Reports what work is unblocked and how far the task is from DoD, and reviews proposed DoD amendments. Read-only on structure. Does NOT implement WUs and does not read every linked file. ([zjteachen/agent-utils](https://github.com/zjteachen/agent-utils/blob/HEAD/agents/overseer.md))
- **overseer-conductor**: Non-interactive/delegated variant of the /overseer conductor for the Cholobots Taiga lifecycle. Never edits files or runs checks — routes all state through Taiga and delegates each unit of work to the right worker agent, reconciles Taiga ground truth, and runs the escalation ladder to Jon. Human gates degrade to stop-and-report (subagents cannot use AskUserQuestion). ([MrsBohemian/Cholobots](https://github.com/MrsBohemian/Cholobots/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/overseer-conductor.md))
- **overseer-team**: Team Overseer — HQ. Project orchestration, architecture decisions, stakeholder communication, team coordination. Balanced, cautious, standards-compliant. ([VarakornUnicornTech/UniOpsQC](https://github.com/VarakornUnicornTech/UniOpsQC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/overseer.md) · ★12)
- **overseer-worker**: Bounded worker for overseer tasks; returns verifiable proof of work ([dheerkt/dotfiles](https://github.com/dheerkt/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/overseer-worker.md))
- **overseer-worker-lite**: Light overseer worker for research, exploration, test runs, and tiny fully specified edits ([dheerkt/dotfiles](https://github.com/dheerkt/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/overseer-worker-lite.md))

The worker took the issue's suggested fix option 1 and wrote the edit directly (agent-definition edits in the HOS source repo are made by the top-level session, not `coder` — CLAUDE.md, #1347): ([thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem](https://github.com/thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem/blob/HEAD/prompts/.claude/agents/overseer.v3.md) · ★4)

- **oversight**: Foundry's oversight agent, the coordinator of a batch from GitHub issues to a merged pull request. Run it as the session's own agent (`claude --agent foundry:oversight`), or start the same procedure with /foundry:begin. It picks a batch at intake, writes the plan, runs the docs pass and the six reviewer sub-agents round by round, builds and tests, opens the pull request, and stops at every point where the maintainer decides. ([brylie/foundry](https://github.com/brylie/foundry/blob/HEAD/agents/oversight.md))
- **oversight**: Checks that the evidence for a disputed Safenet request isn't fabricated: it verifies the facts of a `detective` report with `arbot` in its own way, checks that each fact in the `prosecutor` case is one the `detective` collected, and each cited precedent against `corpus/`. Use it after the `prosecutor` writes a case, before the `judge`. Pass it the paths of the report, the request that `arbot info -json` wrote, the disputed rules, and the case, and the path to write the findings to. ([safe-research/safenet-arbitration-bot](https://github.com/safe-research/safenet-arbitration-bot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight.md))
- **oversight**: You check that the evidence for a disputed Safenet request isn't fabricated: that the facts a `detective` reported are true, and that the facts a `prosecutor` built its case on are ones a `detective` collected. The `judge` that reviews the case next takes its evidence as accurate and its precedents as cited, so you make sure that they are. You don't judge the argument or the verdict, which is for ([safe-research/safenet-arbitration-bot](https://github.com/safe-research/safenet-arbitration-bot/blob/HEAD/agents/oversight.md))
- **oversight-acceptor**: OmniSight 验收 Agent。对照预先声明的验收标准逐项判定，拥有一票否决权。 验收管「过不过」，不管「好不好」。 触发场景： - 任何交付物进入 Phase E - 修复后的重新验收（必须走完整流程，不是抽查） ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-acceptor.md))
- **oversight-auditor**: 唯讀盲審稽核員。在任何 runtime 改動前或 PR 審查時使用，獨立檢視程式碼與設定，絕不修改任何檔案。 ([nick31525373/agent-governance-starter](https://github.com/nick31525373/agent-governance-starter/blob/HEAD/claude-governance-plugin/agents/oversight-auditor.md))
- **oversight-critic**: OmniSight 批判 Agent。从方法论层面质疑「拆解是否合理」，不评价交付物好坏。 需要检验任务分区是否有结构性错误时使用。 触发场景： - 拆解图已产出，需要检验它的前提 - 多个 Agent 给出相同结论，需要有人找反面证据 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-critic.md))
- **oversight-evaluator**: Runs after all internal reviewers have approved a build step and system tests pass. Reads the sign-off register, second review output, and test declarations for the step. Phase 1: checks compliance (did all required sign-offs happen?). Phase 2: evaluates content quality (convergence failures, critical findings, low confidence). Produces a structured recommendation for oversight-orchestrator. Invoke after system tests pass and before opening a PR. ([thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem](https://github.com/thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-evaluator.md) · ★4)
- **oversight-executor**: OmniSight 执行 Agent。按拆解图中的单个子树执行实质工作，产出交付物。 需要写代码、改文件、跑命令时使用。 触发场景： - 拿到一个 ST 子树，需要实现它 - 需要按验收标准补齐交付物 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-executor.md))
- **oversight-orchestrator**: Acts on the oversight-evaluator's recommendation for a build step. PROCEED: opens the PR, writes the handoff document, prints the panel command. CONDITIONAL_PROCEED: same, but adds "Human Review Required Before Merge" section. ESCALATE: surfaces specific, bounded questions to the human — does NOT open the PR. Invoke after oversight-evaluator produces its recommendation. ([thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem](https://github.com/thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-orchestrator.md) · ★4)
- **oversight-planner**: OmniSight 规划 Agent。把用户需求拆成可并行的子树，为每个子树预先声明验收标准、 产出形态与 token 预算。在派发任何执行/检索 Agent 之前使用。 触发场景： - "拆解这个需求" / "规划任务树" - 用户给出一个复杂任务，需要先分区再派发 - 需要回头重新拆解（L4 换策略） ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-planner.md))
- **oversight-probe**: OmniSight 活性探针。只读工具调用日志，判定目标 Agent 是 HEALTHY_EXPLORING / UNFOCUSED / LOOPING。 允许短生命周期，零产出不算失败。 触发场景： - 收件箱（`node "${UNTILDONE_PAYLOAD}/scripts/watch.mjs" --inbox`）里有 WAITING 但 needs 为空—— 宿主没说清在等什么，需要从 transcript 判断是循环、跑偏，还是真在等一个具体决定 - 某 Agent 连续 3 窗 novelty &lt; 0.2，需要判断是循环还是收窄 注意：STALLED 实测命中率 0.018%（56,809 个滑窗里 10 次），末态从未出现。 不要指望靠它触发——真正的触发源是收件箱里的 WAITING。 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-probe.md))
- **oversight-redteam**: OmniSight 红队 Agent。攻击交付物与结论，构造反例、边界条件、失败场景。 需要检验「这个交付物在什么情况下会崩」时使用。 触发场景： - 交付物已产出，需要在验收前做对抗测试 - 一个结论被多个 Agent 认同，需要有人试图推翻它 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-redteam.md))
- **oversight-relay**: OmniSight 接力 Agent。为目标停滞会话产出一条带具体下一步的接力消息草稿。 本库不能自动投递，草稿由用户复制发送。 触发场景： - scan-sessions.mjs 判定触发接力条件 - 用户说「给那个会话写条接力消息」 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-relay.md))
- **oversight-repair**: OmniSight 修复 Agent。仅在验收不通过或锐评低于阈值时触发。接收验收理由 + 原始交付物 + 锐评意见进行修复。 触发场景： - 验收判不通过 - 锐评分低于阈值且用户要求改进 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-repair.md))
- **oversight-research**: OmniSight 检索 Agent。搜集外部事实、数据、竞品、先例，每条标注来源与可信度。 需要查证宿主能力、官方文档、同类实现时使用。 触发场景： - "查一下 X 的官方行为是什么" - 需要外部证据支撑或推翻一个结论 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-research.md))
- **oversight-resolution**: OmniSight 裁决 Agent。读取分歧双方的原话，判断哪一方证据更强。 无法判断时，给出「需要什么额外证据才能裁决」，而不是猜。 触发场景： - Phase C 冲突登记发现实质性分歧 - 两个 Agent 对同一事实给出相反结论 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-resolution.md))
- **oversight-review**: OmniSight 锐评 Agent。对交付物做不留情面的质量评估，给出 1–10 分与最致命的 3 个问题。 锐评管「好不好」，不管「过不过」。 触发场景： - 交付物已产出，需要质量评分 - 需要知道「如果只改一处，改哪里」 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-review.md))
- **oversight-safety**: OmniSight 安全 Agent。检查接力草稿是否触碰红线、是否与原始需求矛盾。 在草稿交给用户之前运行，独立于 relay Agent。 触发场景： - relay Agent 产出草稿后 ([sqkl520/UntilDone](https://github.com/sqkl520/UntilDone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oversight-safety.md))
- **overskill-app-builder**: Owns an Overskill app from a plain-language description through to a published URL: picks the right create tool, starts the build, waits it out, applies follow-up changes, and publishes once the user has said yes. Invoke for a multi-step Overskill job — "build me X and put it live", "add Y to my app and ship it" — where the work is a sequence of hosted build calls and polling rather than local code.
